=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
On a certain planet, there are N aliens, all of whom are minors.
The i-th alien currently has A_i stones, and will become an adult exactly i years later.
When someone becomes an adult on this planet, every adult who has at least one stone gives exactly one stone as a congratulatory gift to the alien who has just become an adult.
Find how many stones each alien will have after N years.
Assume that no new aliens will be born in the future.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N
A_1 A_2 \ldots A_N

Output

Let B_i be the number of stones owned by the i-th alien after N years. Print B_1, B_2, \ldots, B_N in this order, separated by spaces.

Constraints


- 1 \leq N \leq 5 \times 10^5
- 0 \leq A_i \leq 5 \times 10^5
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

4
5 0 9 3

Sample Output 1

2 0 10 5

Let C_i be the number of stones that the i-th alien has at a given time.
Initially, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (5, 0, 9, 3).
After 1 year, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (5, 0, 9, 3).
After 2 years, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (4, 1, 9, 3).
After 3 years, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (3, 0, 11, 3).
After 4 years, (C_1, C_2, C_3, C_4) = (2, 0, 10, 5).

Sample Input 2

5
4 6 7 2 5

Sample Output 2

0 4 7 4 9

Sample Input 3

10
2 9 1 2 0 4 6 7 1 5

Sample Output 3

0 2 0 0 0 4 7 10 4 10

Use the following starter code to write the final solution.
```python

```

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Let's model process.

At each integer time t=1..N, alien t becomes adult. Let S_t be set of adult aliens before time t? At time t, adults are aliens 1..t-1 (became at previous times), and alien t is becoming adult. For t=1..N:

  For all i < t: C_i = max(C_i - 1, 0) (since all positive adults give 1; if zero no change)
  C_t = A_t + r_t
Need after N, after event N.

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:

    N = data[0]
    A = data[1:]
    # But if we use A = data[1:] copy; okay. Could avoid.
    max_d = 500000 + 2 * N + 5
    freq = [0] * (max_d + 1)
    active = 0

Now, let's revisit the recurrence and active update one more time to ensure no off-by-one in `active -= freq[i]` and `if d > i`.

Let's formalize:
Let S_i = {j < i | D_j >= i}. active_i = |S_i|.
At iteration i:
C_i = A_i + active_i.
D_i = C_i + i = A_i + i + active_i.
Then S_{i+1} = {j < i | D_j >= i+1} ∪ ({i} if D_i >= i+1).
The first set size = active_i - |{j < i | D_j = i}| = active_i - freq_i (where freq counts inserted j<i).
The second indicator = [D_i >= i+1] = [D_i > i].

Before final, let's maybe refine code for readability and performance:
```
import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]
    max_d = 500000 + 2 * N + 10
    freq = [0] * (max_d + 1)
    active = 0
    ans = [0] * N

    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        a = data[i]
        d = a + i + active
        b = d - N
        if b < 0:
            b = 0
        ans[i - 1] = b

        active -= freq[i]
        if d > i:
            active += 1
        freq[d] += 1

    sys.stdout.write(' '.join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    N = data[0]

    max_d = 500000 + 2 * N + 10
    freq = [0] * (max_d + 1)

    active = 0
    ans = [0] * N

    for i in range(1, N + 1):
        a = data[i]

        # D_i = C_i + i, where C_i = A_i + active
        d = a + i + active

        # Final stones = max(D_i - N, 0)
        b = d - N
        if b < 0:
            b = 0
        ans[i - 1] = b

        # Move to the next year:
        # Remove aliens whose D is exactly i, add the new alien if D_i > i.
        active -= freq[i]
        if d > i:
            active += 1
        freq[d] += 1

    sys.stdout.write(' '.join(map(str, ans)))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
